+143|  function formatDate(value) {
+144|    if (!value) {
+145|      return 'Unknown time';
+146|    }
+147|    try {
+148|      return dateFormatter.format(new Date(value));
+149|    } catch {
+150|      return value;
+151|    }
+152|  }
+153|
+154|  function setButtonState(button, disabled) {
+155|    if (button) {
+156|      button.disabled = disabled;
+157|    }
+158|  }
+159|
+160|  function normalizeOffset(offset) {
+161|    return clamp(offset, 0, state.document.text.length);
+162|  }
+163|
+164|  function segmentBoundaries(text, segmenter) {
+165|    const boundaries = [0];
+166|    if (segmenter) {
+167|      for (const part of segmenter.segment(text)) {
+168|        if (part.index !== boundaries[boundaries.length - 1]) {
+169|          boundaries.push(part.index);
+170|        }
+171|      }
+172|    } else {
+173|      let index = 0;
+174|      for (const ch of Array.from(text)) {
+175|        index += ch.length;
+176|        if (index !== boundaries[boundaries.length - 1]) {
+177|          boundaries.push(index);
+178|        }
+179|      }
+180|    }
+181|    if (boundaries[boundaries.length - 1] !== text.length) {
+182|      boundaries.push(text.length);
+183|    }
+184|    return boundaries;
+185|  }
+186|
+187|  function buildDocument(text) {
+188|    const rawLines = text.split('\n');
+189|    const lines = [];
+190|    const wordSegments = [];
+191|    let offset = 0;
+192|
+193|    for (let index = 0; index < rawLines.length; index += 1) {
+194|      const lineText = rawLines[index];
+195|      const lineStart = offset;
+196|      const lineEnd = lineStart + lineText.length;
+197|      const hasBreak = index < rawLines.length - 1;
+198|      const nextStart = lineEnd + (hasBreak ? 1 : 0);
+199|      const graphemeBoundaries = segmentBoundaries(lineText, 
graphemeSegmenter);
+200|
+201|      lines.push({
+202|        index,
+203|        text: lineText,
+204|        start: lineStart,
+205|        end: lineEnd,
+206|        nextStart,
+207|        hasBreak,
+208|        graphemeBoundaries,
+209|        graphemeCount: graphemeBoundaries.length - 1,
+210|      });
+211|
+212|      offset = nextStart;
+213|    }
+214|
+215|    if (wordSegmenter) {
+216|      for (const part of wordSegmenter.segment(text)) {
+217|        wordSegments.push({
+218|          start: part.index,
+219|          end: part.index + part.segment.length,
+220|          isWordLike: Boolean(part.isWordLike),
+221|        });
+222|      }
+223|    } else {
+224|      const regex = /[\p{L}\p{N}_]+/gu;
+225|      let match;
+226|      while ((match = regex.exec(text))) {
+227|        wordSegments.push({
+228|          start: match.index,
+229|          end: match.index + match[0].length,
+230|          isWordLike: true,
+231|        });
+232|      }
+233|    }
+234|
+235|    return {
+236|      text,
+237|      lines,
+238|      wordSegments,
+239|    };
+240|  }
+241|
+242|  function currentLineData(offset = getPrimaryCaretOffset()) {
+243|    const normalized = normalizeOffset(offset);
+244|    const lines = state.document.lines;
+245|    for (let index = 0; index < lines.length; index += 1) {
+246|      const line = lines[index];
+247|      if (normalized < line.nextStart || index === lines.length - 1) {
+248|        return { line, lineIndex: index, offsetWithinLine: 
Math.min(normalized - line.start, line.text.length) };
+249|      }
+250|    }
+251|    const last = lines[lines.length - 1];
+252|    return { line: last, lineIndex: lines.length - 1, offsetWithinLine: 
last ? last.text.length : 0 };
+253|  }
+254|
+255|  function graphemeOffsetFromColumn(line, column) {
+256|    const boundaries = line.graphemeBoundaries;
+257|    const capped = clamp(column, 0, boundaries.length - 1);
+258|    return boundaries[capped];
+259|  }
+260|
+261|  function graphemeColumnFromOffset(line, offsetWithinLine) {
+262|    const boundaries = line.graphemeBoundaries;
+263|    let low = 0;
+264|    let high = boundaries.length - 1;
+265|    while (low < high) {
+266|      const mid = Math.ceil((low + high) / 2);
+267|      if (boundaries[mid] <= offsetWithinLine) {
+268|        low = mid;
+269|      } else {
+270|        high = mid - 1;
+271|      }
+272|    }
+273|    return low;
+274|  }
+275|
+276|  function lineColumnPosition(offset) {
+277|    const { line, lineIndex, offsetWithinLine } = currentLineData(offset);
+278|    return {
+279|      lineIndex,
+280|      column: graphemeColumnFromOffset(line, offsetWithinLine),
+281|      line,
+282|    };
+283|  }
+284|
+285|  function getPrimaryCaretOffset() {
+286|    return normalizeOffset(state.selection.focus);
+287|  }
+288|
+289|  function getSelectionRange() {
+290|    const range = selectionRange();
+291|    return {
+292|      start: normalizeOffset(range.start),
+293|      end: normalizeOffset(range.end),
+294|    };
+295|  }
+296|
+297|  function getAllCaretOffsets() {
+298|    const offsets = [getPrimaryCaretOffset(), 
...state.extraCarets.map(normalizeOffset)];
+299|    return Array.from(new Set(offsets)).sort((a, b) => a - b);
+300|  }
+301|
+302|  function collapseTo(offset, preserveExtras = false) {
+303|    const next = normalizeOffset(offset);
+304|    state.selection = { anchor: next, focus: next };
+305|    if (!preserveExtras) {
+306|      state.extraCarets = [];
+307|    }
+308|    state.preferredColumn = lineColumnPosition(next).column;
+309|  }
+310|
+311|  function selectRange(anchor, focus) {
+312|    state.selection = {
+313|      anchor: normalizeOffset(anchor),
+314|      focus: normalizeOffset(focus),
+315|    };
+316|    state.extraCarets = [];
+317|    state.preferredColumn = 
lineColumnPosition(state.selection.focus).column;
+318|  }
+319|
+320|  function applySelectionAfterEdit(nextCarets, preferredColumn = null) {
+321|    const sorted = Array.from(new 
Set(nextCarets.map(normalizeOffset))).sort((a, b) => a - b);
+322|    if (sorted.length === 0) {
+323|      collapseTo(0);
+324|      return;
+325|    }
+326|    state.selection = { anchor: sorted[0], focus: sorted[0] };
+327|    state.extraCarets = sorted.slice(1);
+328|    state.preferredColumn = preferredColumn === null ? 
lineColumnPosition(sorted[0]).column : preferredColumn;
+329|  }
+330|
+331|  function pushUndoSnapshot() {
+332|    state.undoStack.push({
+333|      text: state.text,
+334|      selection: cloneSelection(state.selection),
+335|      extraCarets: [...state.extraCarets],
+336|      preferredColumn: state.preferredColumn,
+337|    });
+338|    if (state.undoStack.length > 100) {
+339|      state.undoStack.shift();
+340|    }
+341|  }
+342|
+343|  function restoreSnapshot(snapshot) {
+344|    state.text = snapshot.text;
+345|    state.document = buildDocument(state.text);
+346|    state.selection = cloneSelection(snapshot.selection);
+347|    state.extraCarets = [...snapshot.extraCarets];
+348|    state.preferredColumn = snapshot.preferredColumn;
+349|    state.dirty = state.text !== state.savedText;
+350|    state.editSession = null;
+351|    updateMatchesAfterTextChange(false);
+352|    renderAll();
+353|  }
+354|
+355|  function beginEditSession(kind) {
+356|    const now = Date.now();
+357|    const session = state.editSession;
+358|    if (!session || session.kind !== kind || now - session.startedAt > 700)
{
+359|      pushUndoSnapshot();
+360|      state.redoStack = [];
+361|      state.editSession = { kind, startedAt: now };
+362|    } else {
+363|      session.startedAt = now;
+364|    }
+365|  }
+366|
+367|  function endEditSession() {
+368|    state.editSession = null;
+369|  }
+370|
+371|  function replaceText(nextText, nextSelection, kind) {
+372|    beginEditSession(kind);
+373|    state.text = nextText;
+374|    state.document = buildDocument(nextText);
+375|    state.selection = {
+376|      anchor: normalizeOffset(nextSelection.anchor),
+377|      focus: normalizeOffset(nextSelection.focus),
+378|    };
+379|    state.extraCarets = nextSelection.extraCarets ? 
[...nextSelection.extraCarets] : [];
+380|    state.preferredColumn = nextSelection.preferredColumn !== undefined
+381|      ? nextSelection.preferredColumn
+382|      : lineColumnPosition(state.selection.focus).column;
+383|    state.dirty = state.text !== state.savedText;
+384|    updateMatchesAfterTextChange(true);
+385|    renderAll();
+386|  }
+387|
+388|  function replaceRangeInText(text, start, end, replacement) {
+389|    return text.slice(0, start) + replacement + text.slice(end);
+390|  }
+391|
+392|  function applyBatchEdits(edits, kind) {
+393|    if (!edits.length) {
+394|      return;
+395|    }
+396|    const sorted = [...edits].sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start || a.end - 
b.end);
+397|    beginEditSession(kind);
+398|    let nextText = state.text;
+399|    let shift = 0;
+400|    const nextCarets = [];
+401|
+402|    for (const edit of sorted) {
+403|      const start = normalizeOffset(edit.start + shift);
+404|      const end = normalizeOffset(edit.end + shift);
+405|      nextText = replaceRangeInText(nextText, start, end, edit.insert);
+406|      shift += edit.insert.length - (edit.end - edit.start);
+407|      nextCarets.push(start + edit.insert.length);
+408|    }
+409|
+410|    state.text = nextText;
+411|    state.document = buildDocument(nextText);
+412|    applySelectionAfterEdit(nextCarets, sorted.length ? null : 
state.preferredColumn);
+413|    state.dirty = state.text !== state.savedText;
+414|    updateMatchesAfterTextChange(true);
+415|    renderAll();
+416|  }
+417|
+418|  function deleteRange(start, end, kind) {
+419|    if (start === end) {
+420|      return;
+421|    }
+422|    replaceText(
+423|      replaceRangeInText(state.text, start, end, ''),
+424|      { anchor: start, focus: start },
+425|      kind,
+426|    );
+427|  }
+428|
+429|  function insertText(text, kind = 'typing') {
+430|    if (!text) {
+431|      return;
+432|    }
+433|
+434|    if (!selectionIsCollapsed() && state.extraCarets.length === 0) {
+435|      const range = getSelectionRange();
+436|      replaceText(
+437|        replaceRangeInText(state.text, range.start, range.end, text),
+438|        { anchor: range.start + text.length, focus: range.start + 
text.length },
+439|        kind,
+440|      );
+441|      return;
+442|    }
+443|
+444|    const offsets = getAllCaretOffsets();
+445|    const edits = offsets.map((offset) => ({ start: offset, end: offset, 
insert: text }));
+446|    applyBatchEdits(edits, kind);
+447|  }
+448|
+449|  function deleteBackward(kind = 'delete') {
+450|    if (!selectionIsCollapsed() && state.extraCarets.length === 0) {
+451|      const range = getSelectionRange();
+452|      deleteRange(range.start, range.end, kind);
+453|      return;
+454|    }
+455|
+456|    const edits = [];
+457|    for (const offset of getAllCaretOffsets()) {
+458|      const prev = previousDeletionBoundary(offset);
+459|      if (prev < offset) {
+460|        edits.push({ start: prev, end: offset, insert: '' });
+461|      }
+462|    }
+463|    applyBatchEdits(edits, kind);
+464|  }
+465|
+466|  function deleteForward(kind = 'delete') {
+467|    if (!selectionIsCollapsed() && state.extraCarets.length === 0) {
+468|      const range = getSelectionRange();
+469|      deleteRange(range.start, range.end, kind);
+470|      return;
+471|    }
+472|
+473|    const edits = [];
+474|    for (const offset of getAllCaretOffsets()) {
+475|      const next = nextDeletionBoundary(offset);
+476|      if (next > offset) {
+477|        edits.push({ start: offset, end: next, insert: '' });
+478|      }
+479|    }
+480|    applyBatchEdits(edits, kind);
+481|  }
+482|
+483|  function previousDeletionBoundary(offset) {
+484|    const pos = lineColumnPosition(offset);
+485|    const line = pos.line;
+486|    const column = pos.column;
+487|    if (column > 0) {
+488|      return line.start + graphemeOffsetFromColumn(line, column - 1);
+489|    }
+490|    if (pos.lineIndex === 0) {
+491|      return offset;
+492|    }
+493|    const previousLine = state.document.lines[pos.lineIndex - 1];
+494|    return previousLine.end;
+495|  }
+496|
+497|  function nextDeletionBoundary(offset) {
+498|    const pos = lineColumnPosition(offset);
+499|    const line = pos.line;
+500|    const column = pos.column;
+501|    if (column < line.graphemeCount) {
+502|      return line.start + graphemeOffsetFromColumn(line, column + 1);
+503|    }
+504|    if (pos.lineIndex === state.document.lines.length - 1) {
+505|      return offset;
+506|    }
+507|    return line.nextStart;
+508|  }
+509|
+510|  function moveSelection(direction, withShift = false, byWord = false, 
byLine = false) {
+511|    endEditSession();
+512|    state.extraCarets = [];
+513|
+514|    const current = withShift ? state.selection.focus : (direction < 0 ? 
getSelectionRange().start : getSelectionRange().end);
+515|    const next = byWord
+516|      ? moveByWord(current, direction)
+517|      : byLine
+518|        ? moveByLine(current, direction)
+519|        : moveByCharacter(current, direction);
+520|
+521|    if (withShift) {
+522|      state.selection.focus = next;
+523|    } else {
+524|      state.selection = { anchor: next, focus: next };
+525|    }
+526|    state.preferredColumn = lineColumnPosition(next).column;
+527|    renderAll();
+528|    scrollPrimaryCaretIntoView();
+529|  }
+530|
+531|  function moveByCharacter(offset, direction) {
+532|    const pos = lineColumnPosition(offset);
+533|    if (direction < 0) {
+534|      if (pos.column > 0) {
+535|        return pos.line.start + graphemeOffsetFromColumn(pos.line, 
pos.column - 1);
+536|      }
+537|      if (pos.lineIndex === 0) {
+538|        return 0;
+539|      }
+540|      const previousLine = state.document.lines[pos.lineIndex - 1];
+541|      return previousLine.end;
+542|    }
+543|
+544|    if (pos.column < pos.line.graphemeCount) {
+545|      return pos.line.start + graphemeOffsetFromColumn(pos.line, pos.column
+ 1);
+546|    }
+547|    if (pos.lineIndex === state.document.lines.length - 1) {
+548|      return state.document.text.length;
+549|    }
+550|    return pos.line.nextStart;
+551|  }
+552|
+553|  function moveByLine(offset, direction) {
+554|    const current = lineColumnPosition(offset);
+555|    const targetIndex = clamp(current.lineIndex + direction, 0, 
state.document.lines.length - 1);
+556|    const targetLine = state.document.lines[targetIndex];
+557|    const targetColumn = clamp(state.preferredColumn, 0, 
targetLine.graphemeCount);
+558|    return targetLine.start + graphemeOffsetFromColumn(targetLine, 
targetColumn);
+559|  }
+560|
+561|  function moveByWord(offset, direction) {
+562|    const segments = state.document.wordSegments;
+563|    if (!segments.length) {
+564|      return direction < 0 ? 0 : state.document.text.length;
+565|    }
+566|
+567|    if (direction > 0) {
+568|      for (const segment of segments) {
+569|        if (segment.start > offset) {
+570|          return segment.start;
+571|        }
+572|        if (segment.start <= offset && segment.end > offset && 
segment.isWordLike) {
+573|          return segment.end;
+574|        }
+575|      }
+576|      return state.document.text.length;
+577|    }
+578|
+579|    for (let index = segments.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
+580|      const segment = segments[index];
+581|      if (segment.end < offset) {
+582|        return segment.isWordLike ? segment.start : segment.start;
+583|      }
+584|      if (segment.start < offset && segment.end >= offset && 
segment.isWordLike) {
+585|        return segment.start;
+586|      }
+587|    }
+588|    return 0;
+589|  }
+590|
+591|  function selectAll() {
+592|    endEditSession();
+593|    state.selection = { anchor: 0, focus: state.document.text.length };
+594|    state.extraCarets = [];
+595|    state.preferredColumn = 0;
+596|    renderAll();
+597|    scrollPrimaryCaretIntoView();
+598|  }
+599|
+600|  function indentSelection(unindent = false) {
+601|    endEditSession();
+602|    const range = getSelectionRange();
+603|    const lines = state.document.lines;
+604|    const firstLineIndex = currentLineData(range.start).lineIndex;
+605|    const lastLineIndex = currentLineData(Math.max(range.end - 1, 
0)).lineIndex;
+606|    const edits = [];
+607|    let anchorShift = 0;
+608|    let focusShift = 0;
+609|
+610|    for (let index = firstLineIndex; index <= lastLineIndex; index += 1) {
+611|      const line = lines[index];
+612|      if (unindent) {
+613|        if (line.text.startsWith('\t')) {
+614|          edits.push({ start: line.start, end: line.start + 1, insert: '' 
});
+615|          if (line.start < range.start) anchorShift -= 1;
+616|          if (line.start < range.end) focusShift -= 1;
+617|        } else if (line.text.startsWith('  ')) {
+618|          edits.push({ start: line.start, end: line.start + 2, insert: '' 
});
+619|          if (line.start < range.start) anchorShift -= 2;
+620|          if (line.start < range.end) focusShift -= 2;
+621|        }
+622|      } else {
+623|        edits.push({ start: line.start, end: line.start, insert: '\t' });
+624|        if (line.start <= range.start) anchorShift += 1;
+625|        if (line.start <= range.end) focusShift += 1;
+626|      }
+627|    }
+628|
+629|    if (!edits.length) {
+630|      return;
+631|    }
+632|
+633|    const sorted = edits.sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start);
+634|    let nextText = state.text;
+635|    let shift = 0;
+636|    for (const edit of sorted) {
+637|      const start = edit.start + shift;
+638|      const end = edit.end + shift;
+639|      nextText = replaceRangeInText(nextText, start, end, edit.insert);
+640|      shift += edit.insert.length - (edit.end - edit.start);
+641|    }
+642|
+643|    replaceText(
+644|      nextText,
+645|      {
+646|        anchor: range.start + anchorShift,
+647|        focus: range.end + focusShift,
+648|      },
+649|      unindent ? 'unindent' : 'indent',
+650|    );
+651|  }
+652|
+653|  function copySelectionToClipboard(cut = false) {
+654|    const range = getSelectionRange();
+655|    if (range.start === range.end || state.extraCarets.length) {
+656|      return false;
+657|    }
+658|    const text = state.text.slice(range.start, range.end);
+659|    return writeClipboardText(text).then(() => {
+660|      if (cut) {
+661|        deleteRange(range.start, range.end, 'cut');
+662|      }
+663|    });
+664|  }
+665|
+666|  async function writeClipboardText(text) {
+667|    try {
+668|      if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {
+669|        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
+670|        return true;
+671|      }
+672|    } catch {
+673|      // Fallback below.
+674|    }
+675|    return false;
+676|  }
+677|
+678|  function isEditorFocused() {
+679|    return document.activeElement === els.editorViewport || 
els.editorViewport.contains(document.activeElement);
+680|  }
+681|
+682|  function navigateMatches(direction) {
+683|    if (!state.findQuery) {
+684|      setFeedback('Enter a search term before navigating matches.', 
'warning');
+685|      return;
+686|    }
+687|    if (!state.matches.length) {
+688|      setFeedback(`No matches found for “${state.findQuery}”.`, 'warning');
+689|      return;
+690|    }
+691|    if (state.activeMatchIndex < 0) {
+692|      state.activeMatchIndex = direction > 0 ? 0 : state.matches.length - 
1;
+693|    } else {
+694|      state.activeMatchIndex = (state.activeMatchIndex + direction + 
state.matches.length) % state.matches.length;
+695|    }
+696|    activateMatch(state.activeMatchIndex, true);
+697|  }
+698|
+699|  function activateMatch(index, scrollIntoView = false) {
+700|    if (!state.matches.length) {
+701|      return;
+702|    }
+703|    const match = state.matches[clamp(index, 0, state.matches.length - 1)];
+704|    if (!match) {
+705|      return;
+706|    }
+707|    state.activeMatchIndex = index;
+708|    state.selection = { anchor: match.start, focus: match.end };
+709|    state.extraCarets = [];
+710|    state.preferredColumn = lineColumnPosition(match.end).column;
+711|    renderAll();
+712|    if (scrollIntoView) {
+713|      scrollPrimaryCaretIntoView();
+714|    }
+715|  }
+716|
+717|  function refreshFindMatches(selectFirst = false) {
+718|    const query = state.findQuery;
+719|    if (!query) {
+720|      state.matches = [];
+721|      state.activeMatchIndex = -1;
+722|      renderFindCount();
+723|      return;
+724|    }
+725|
+726|    const matches = [];
+727|    let startIndex = 0;
+728|    while (startIndex <= state.text.length) {
+729|      const found = state.text.indexOf(query, startIndex);
+730|      if (found === -1) {
+731|        break;
+732|      }
+733|      matches.push({ start: found, end: found + query.length });
+734|      startIndex = found + Math.max(1, query.length);
+735|    }
+736|
+737|    state.matches = matches;
+738|    if (!matches.length) {
+739|      state.activeMatchIndex = -1;
+740|      renderFindCount();
+741|      return;
+742|    }
+743|
+744|    if (selectFirst || state.activeMatchIndex < 0 || state.activeMatchIndex
>= matches.length) {
+745|      state.activeMatchIndex = 0;
+746|    }
+747|    const active = matches[state.activeMatchIndex];
+748|    state.selection = { anchor: active.start, focus: active.end };
+749|    state.extraCarets = [];
+750|    state.preferredColumn = lineColumnPosition(active.end).column;
+751|    renderFindCount();
+752|  }
+753|
+754|  function updateMatchesAfterTextChange(selectFirst) {
+755|    if (!state.findQuery) {
+756|      state.matches = [];
+757|      state.activeMatchIndex = -1;
+758|      renderFindCount();
+759|      return;
+760|    }
+761|    refreshFindMatches(selectFirst);
+762|  }
+763|
+764|  function replaceCurrentMatch() {
+765|    if (!state.findQuery) {
+766|      setFeedback('Type a search term before replacing a match.', 
'warning');
+767|      return;
+768|    }
+769|    if (!state.matches.length) {
+770|      setFeedback(`No matches found for “${state.findQuery}”.`, 'warning');
+771|      return;
+772|    }
+773|    if (state.activeMatchIndex < 0) {
+774|      state.activeMatchIndex = 0;
+775|    }
+776|    const match = state.matches[state.activeMatchIndex];
+777|    replaceText(
+778|      replaceRangeInText(state.text, match.start, match.end, 
els.replaceInput.value),
+779|      { anchor: match.start + els.replaceInput.value.length, focus: 
match.start + els.replaceInput.value.length },
+780|      'replace',
+781|    );
+782|    refreshFindMatches(false);
+783|    if (state.matches.length) {
+784|      state.activeMatchIndex = clamp(state.activeMatchIndex, 0, 
state.matches.length - 1);
+785|      activateMatch(state.activeMatchIndex, false);
+786|    }
+787|  }
+788|
+789|  function replaceAllMatches() {
+790|    if (!state.findQuery) {
+791|      setFeedback('Type a search term before replacing matches.', 
'warning');
+792|      return;
+793|    }
+794|    if (!state.matches.length) {
+795|      setFeedback(`No matches found for “${state.findQuery}”.`, 'warning');
+796|      return;
+797|    }
+798|    const replacement = els.replaceInput.value;
+799|    const query = state.findQuery;
+800|    const matches = [...state.matches];
+801|    let nextText = state.text;
+802|    for (let index = matches.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
+803|      const match = matches[index];
+804|      nextText = replaceRangeInText(nextText, match.start, match.end, 
replacement);
+805|    }
+806|    const first = matches[0];
+807|    replaceText(nextText, { anchor: first.start, focus: first.start }, 
'replaceAll');
+808|    refreshFindMatches(true);
+809|    setFeedback(`Replaced ${matches.length} ${matches.length === 1 ? 
'match' : 'matches'} for “${query}”.`, 'success');
+810|  }
+811|
+812|  function setPreview(revision, content, createdAt) {
+813|    state.previewRevision = revision;
+814|    state.preview = { revision, content, createdAt };
+815|    renderPreview();
+816|  }
+817|
+818|  async function previewRevision(revision) {
+819|    if (!state.report) {
+820|      return;
+821|    }
+822|    try {
+823|      const response = await 
fetch(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(state.report.id)}/revisions/${revision}
`);
+824|      if (!response.ok) {
+825|        throw new Error(`Failed to load revision ${revision}`);
+826|      }
+827|      const payload = await response.json();
+828|      const selected = payload.revision;
+829|      setPreview(selected.revision, selected.content, selected.createdAt);
+830|      setFeedback(`Previewing revision ${selected.revision}.`, 'info');
+831|    } catch (error) {
+832|      setFeedback(error.message || 'Unable to preview revision.', 'error');
+833|    }
+834|  }
+835|
+836|  async function restoreRevision(revision) {
+837|    if (!state.report) {
+838|      return;
+839|    }
+840|    try {
+841|      const response = await 
fetch(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(state.report.id)}/revisions/${revision}
`);
+842|      if (!response.ok) {
+843|        throw new Error(`Failed to restore revision ${revision}`);
+844|      }
+845|      const payload = await response.json();
+846|      const selected = payload.revision;
+847|      if (selected.content === state.text) {
+848|        setFeedback(`Revision ${selected.revision} is already open.`, 
'info');
+849|        return;
+850|      }
+851|      replaceText(
+852|        selected.content,
+853|        { anchor: 0, focus: 0 },
+854|        'restore',
+855|      );
+856|      collapseTo(0);
+857|      renderAll();
+858|      scrollPrimaryCaretIntoView();
+859|      setFeedback(`Restored revision ${selected.revision} as an unsaved 
edit.`, 'success');
+860|    } catch (error) {
+861|      setFeedback(error.message || 'Unable to restore revision.', 'error');
+862|    }
+863|  }
+864|
+865|  async function openReport(reportId, options = {}) {
+866|    if (!reportId) {
+867|      return;
+868|    }
+869|    if (state.report && state.report.id !== reportId && state.dirty && 
!options.force) {
+870|      const ok = window.confirm('Switching reports will discard unsaved 
changes. Continue?');
+871|      if (!ok) {
+872|        return;
+873|      }
+874|    }
+875|
+876|    state.loading = true;
+877|    renderButtons();
+878|    try {
+879|      const [reportResponse, historyResponse] = await Promise.all([
+880|        fetch(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(reportId)}`),
+881|        fetch(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(reportId)}/revisions`),
+882|      ]);
+883|
+884|      if (!reportResponse.ok) {
+885|        throw new Error('Report could not be loaded.');
+886|      }
+887|      if (!historyResponse.ok) {
+888|        throw new Error('Revision history could not be loaded.');
+889|      }
+890|
+891|      const reportPayload = await reportResponse.json();
+892|      const historyPayload = await historyResponse.json();
+893|      const report = reportPayload.report;
+894|
+895|      state.report = report;
+896|      state.selectedReportId = report.id;
+897|      state.text = report.content;
+898|      state.savedText = report.content;
+899|      state.baseRevision = report.currentRevision;
+900|      state.savedRevision = report.currentRevision;
+901|      state.latestServerRevision = report.currentRevision;
+902|      state.loadedAt = report.updatedAt;
+903|      state.dirty = false;
+904|      state.selection = { anchor: 0, focus: 0 };
+905|      state.extraCarets = [];
+906|      state.undoStack = [];
+907|      state.redoStack = [];
+908|      state.editSession = null;
+909|      state.document = buildDocument(state.text);
+910|      state.preview = null;
+911|      state.previewRevision = null;
+912|      state.history = historyPayload.revisions;
+913|      state.matches = [];
+914|      state.activeMatchIndex = -1;
+915|      state.preferredColumn = 0;
+916|      clearFeedback();
+917|      refreshFindMatches(false);
+918|      renderAll();
+919|      els.editorViewport.focus();
+920|      scrollPrimaryCaretIntoView(true);
+921|      setFeedback(`Opened ${report.title} at revision 
${report.currentRevision}.`, 'success');
+922|    } catch (error) {
+923|      setFeedback(error.message || 'Unable to open report.', 'error');
+924|    } finally {
+925|      state.loading = false;
+926|      renderButtons();
+927|    }
+928|  }
+929|
+930|  async function reloadReport() {
+931|    if (!state.report) {
+932|      return;
+933|    }
+934|    if (state.dirty) {
+935|      const ok = window.confirm('Reloading will discard your unsaved draft.
Continue?');
+936|      if (!ok) {
+937|        return;
+938|      }
+939|    }
+940|    await openReport(state.report.id, { force: true });
+941|    setFeedback(`Reloaded the saved version of ${state.report.title}.`, 
'success');
+942|  }
+943|
+944|  async function discardDraft() {
+945|    if (!state.report) {
+946|      return;
+947|    }
+948|    if (!state.dirty) {
+949|      setFeedback('There are no local changes to discard.', 'info');
+950|      return;
+951|    }
+952|    const ok = window.confirm('Discard your unsaved changes and return to 
the saved version?');
+953|    if (!ok) {
+954|      return;
+955|    }
+956|    await openReport(state.report.id, { force: true });
+957|    setFeedback(`Discarded local changes for ${state.report.title}.`, 
'success');
+958|  }
+959|
+960|  async function saveReport() {
+961|    if (!state.report) {
+962|      return;
+963|    }
+964|    if (!state.dirty) {
+965|      setFeedback('Nothing has changed since the last save.', 'info');
+966|      return;
+967|    }
+968|
+969|    try {
+970|      const response = await 
fetch(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(state.report.id)}/save`, {
+971|        method: 'POST',
+972|        headers: {
+973|          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
+974|        },
+975|        body: JSON.stringify({
+976|          documentId: state.report.id,
+977|          baseRevision: state.baseRevision,
+978|          content: state.text,
+979|        }),
+980|      });
+981|
+982|      const payload = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
+983|      if (response.status === 409) {
+984|        const latest = payload.report;
+985|        state.latestServerRevision = latest ? latest.currentRevision : 
state.latestServerRevision;
+986|        renderAll();
+987|        setFeedback(
+988|          `Save rejected because the server is now at revision ${latest ? 
latest.currentRevision : 'a newer revision'}. Your draft is still محفوظ.`,
+989|          'error',
+990|        );
+991|        return;
+992|      }
+993|
+994|      if (!response.ok) {
+995|        throw new Error(payload.error || 'Save failed.');
+996|      }
+997|
+998|      const report = payload.report;
+999|      state.report = report;
+1000|      state.savedText = report.content;
+1001|      state.text = report.content;
+1002|      state.baseRevision = report.currentRevision;
+1003|      state.savedRevision = report.currentRevision;
+1004|      state.latestServerRevision = report.currentRevision;
+1005|      state.loadedAt = report.updatedAt;
+1006|      state.dirty = false;
+1007|      state.selection = { anchor: state.selection.focus, focus: 
state.selection.focus };
+1008|      state.extraCarets = [];
+1009|      state.undoStack = [];
+1010|      state.redoStack = [];
+1011|      state.editSession = null;
+1012|      state.document = buildDocument(state.text);
+1013|      await loadHistory(report.id);
+1014|      refreshFindMatches(false);
+1015|      renderAll();
+1016|      setFeedback(`Saved revision ${report.currentRevision}.`, 'success');
+1017|    } catch (error) {
+1018|      setFeedback(error.message || 'Unable to save report.', 'error');
+1019|    }
+1020|  }
+1021|
+1022|  async function loadHistory(reportId) {
+1023|    const response = await 
fetch(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(reportId)}/revisions`);
+1024|    if (!response.ok) {
+1025|      throw new Error('Revision history could not be loaded.');
+1026|    }
+1027|    const payload = await response.json();
+1028|    state.history = payload.revisions;
+1029|    renderHistory();
+1030|  }
+1031|
+1032|  async function openPreviewFromHistory(revision) {
+1033|    state.previewRevision = revision;
+1034|    renderHistory();
+1035|    await previewRevision(revision);
+1036|  }
+1037|
+1038|  function onFindInput() {
+1039|    state.findQuery = els.findInput.value;
+1040|    refreshFindMatches(true);
+1041|    renderAll();
+1042|    if (state.findQuery && state.matches.length) {
+1043|      setFeedback(`Found ${state.matches.length} ${state.matches.length 
=== 1 ? 'match' : 'matches'}.`, 'info');
+1044|    } else if (state.findQuery) {
+1045|      setFeedback(`No matches found for “${state.findQuery}”.`, 
'warning');
+1046|    } else {
+1047|      setFeedback('Search cleared.', 'info');
+1048|    }
+1049|  }
+1050|
+1051|  function renderFeedback() {
+1052|    if (!els.feedback) {
+1053|      return;
+1054|    }
+1055|    els.feedback.className = `feedback ${state.messageKind === 'error' ? 
'is-error' : state.messageKind === 'warning' ? 'is-warning' : state.messageKind 
=== 'success' ? 'is-success' : ''}`.trim();
+1056|    els.feedback.textContent = state.message || 'Ready.';
+1057|    renderSaveState();
+1058|  }
+1059|
+1060|  function renderSaveState() {
+1061|    if (!els.saveState) {
+1062|      return;
+1063|    }
+1064|    els.saveState.className = 'status-pill';
+1065|    if (state.loading) {
+1066|      els.saveState.textContent = 'Loading…';
+1067|      return;
+1068|    }
+1069|    if (!state.report) {
+1070|      els.saveState.textContent = 'No report open';
+1071|      return;
+1072|    }
+1073|
+1074|    if (state.dirty) {
+1075|      const stale = state.savedRevision !== state.baseRevision || 
state.latestServerRevision > state.baseRevision;
+1076|      els.saveState.textContent = stale
+1077|        ? `Unsaved draft · base ${state.baseRevision} / server 
${Math.max(state.savedRevision, state.latestServerRevision)}`
+1078|        : 'Unsaved changes';
+1079|      els.saveState.classList.add(stale ? 'is-error' : 'is-dirty');
+1080|      return;
+1081|    }
+1082|
+1083|    els.saveState.textContent = `Saved · revision ${state.savedRevision}`;
+1084|    els.saveState.classList.add('is-saved');
+1085|  }
+1086|
+1087|  function renderCursorState() {
+1088|    if (!els.cursorState) {
+1089|      return;
+1090|    }
+1091|    if (!state.report) {
+1092|      els.cursorState.textContent = 'Open a report to begin editing.';
+1093|      return;
+1094|    }
+1095|
+1096|    const primary = lineColumnPosition(getPrimaryCaretOffset());
+1097|    const selectedChars = selectionLength();
+1098|    const caretCount = 1 + state.extraCarets.length;
+1099|    const parts = [`Line ${primary.lineIndex + 1}`, `column 
${primary.column + 1}`];
+1100|    if (caretCount > 1) {
+1101|      parts.push(`${caretCount} carets`);
+1102|    }
+1103|    if (selectedChars > 0) {
+1104|      parts.push(`${selectedChars} selected`);
+1105|    }
+1106|    els.cursorState.textContent = parts.join(' · ');
+1107|  }
+1108|
+1109|  function renderReportHeader() {
+1110|    if (!state.report) {
+1111|      els.reportTitle.textContent = 'Northwind API Incident Report';
+1112|      els.reportMeta.textContent = 'Open a report to see its saved 
content.';
+1113|      return;
+1114|    }
+1115|    els.reportTitle.textContent = state.report.title;
+1116|    els.reportMeta.textContent = `ID ${state.report.id} · Author 
${state.report.author} · Revision ${state.savedRevision} · Updated 
${formatDate(state.loadedAt)}${state.report.summary ? ` · 
${state.report.summary}` : ''}`;
+1117|  }
+1118|
+1119|  function renderReportList() {
+1120|    if (!els.reportList) {
+1121|      return;
+1122|    }
+1123|    const buttons = 
[...els.reportList.querySelectorAll('[data-report-id]')];
+1124|    for (const button of buttons) {
+1125|      const reportId = button.getAttribute('data-report-id');
+1126|      const report = state.reports.find((entry) => entry.id === reportId);
+1127|      button.classList.toggle('is-active', state.report && state.report.id
=== reportId);
+1128|      if (report) {
+1129|        button.setAttribute('aria-pressed', state.report && 
state.report.id === reportId ? 'true' : 'false');
+1130|      }
+1131|      if (!button.dataset.bound) {
+1132|        button.addEventListener('click', () => {
+1133|          openReport(reportId);
+1134|        });
+1135|        button.dataset.bound = 'true';
+1136|      }
+1137|    }
+1138|  }
+1139|
+1140|  function renderHistory() {
+1141|    if (!els.historyList) {
+1142|      return;
+1143|    }
+1144|    if (!state.report) {
+1145|      els.historyList.innerHTML = '<div class="empty-note">Open a report 
to see its revisions.</div>';
+1146|      return;
+1147|    }
+1148|
+1149|    if (!state.history || !state.history.length) {
+1150|      els.historyList.innerHTML = '<div class="empty-note">No revisions 
are stored yet.</div>';
+1151|      return;
+1152|    }
+1153|
+1154|    els.historyList.innerHTML = state.history.map((revision) => {
+1155|      const active = state.previewRevision === revision.revision;
+1156|      return `
+1157|        <div class="history-item ${active ? 'is-active' : ''}" 
data-history-revision="${revision.revision}">
+1158|          <button type="button" class="history-item__title-button" 
data-preview-revision="${revision.revision}">
+1159|            <span class="history-item__title">Revision 
${revision.revision}${revision.current ? ' · current' : ''}</span>
+1160|          </button>
+1161|          <div 
class="history-item__meta">${escapeHtml(formatDate(revision.createdAt))} · 
${revision.contentLength} chars</div>
+1162|          <div class="history-actions">
+1163|            <button type="button" 
data-restore-revision="${revision.revision}">Restore</button>
+1164|          </div>
+1165|        </div>
+1166|      `;
+1167|    }).join('');
+1168|
+1169|    for (const entry of 
els.historyList.querySelectorAll('[data-preview-revision]')) {
+1170|      if (entry.dataset.bound) {
+1171|        continue;
+1172|      }
+1173|      entry.addEventListener('click', () => {
+1174|        
openPreviewFromHistory(Number(entry.getAttribute('data-preview-revision')));
+1175|      });
+1176|      entry.dataset.bound = 'true';
+1177|    }
+1178|
+1179|    for (const entry of 
els.historyList.querySelectorAll('[data-restore-revision]')) {
+1180|      if (entry.dataset.bound) {
+1181|        continue;
+1182|      }
+1183|      entry.addEventListener('click', () => {
+1184|        
restoreRevision(Number(entry.getAttribute('data-restore-revision')));
+1185|      });
+1186|      entry.dataset.bound = 'true';
+1187|    }
+1188|  }
+1189|
+1190|  function renderPreview() {
+1191|    if (!els.previewPane) {
+1192|      return;
+1193|    }
+1194|    if (!state.preview) {
+1195|      els.previewPane.innerHTML = '<div class="empty-note">Select a 
revision to preview it.</div>';
+1196|      return;
+1197|    }
+1198|    els.previewPane.innerHTML = `
+1199|      <div class="preview-meta">Revision ${state.preview.revision} · 
${escapeHtml(formatDate(state.preview.createdAt))}</div>
+1200|      <pre>${escapeHtml(state.preview.content)}</pre>
+1201|    `;
+1202|  }
+1203|
+1204|  function renderFindCount() {
+1205|    if (!els.matchCount) {
+1206|      return;
+1207|    }
+1208|    if (!state.findQuery) {
+1209|      els.matchCount.textContent = '0 matches';
+1210|      return;
+1211|    }
+1212|    els.matchCount.textContent = `${state.matches.length} 
${state.matches.length === 1 ? 'match' : 'matches'}`;
+1213|  }
+1214|
+1215|  function renderButtons() {
+1216|    setButtonState(els.saveButton, !state.report || state.loading);
+1217|    setButtonState(els.undoButton, !state.undoStack.length || 
state.loading);
+1218|    setButtonState(els.redoButton, !state.redoStack.length || 
state.loading);
+1219|    setButtonState(els.findNextButton, !state.report || state.loading);
+1220|    setButtonState(els.findPrevButton, !state.report || state.loading);
+1221|    setButtonState(els.replaceCurrentButton, !state.report || 
state.loading);
+1222|    setButtonState(els.replaceAllButton, !state.report || state.loading);
+1223|    setButtonState(els.reloadButton, !state.report || state.loading);
+1224|    setButtonState(els.discardButton, !state.report || state.loading);
+1225|  }
+1226|
+1227|  function updateLayoutMetrics() {
+1228|    const style = getComputedStyle(els.editorInner);
+1229|    const fontSize = parseFloat(style.fontSize) || 14;
+1230|    const lineHeight = parseFloat(style.lineHeight) || 24;
+1231|    state.layout.paddingLeft = parseFloat(style.paddingLeft) || 16;
+1232|    state.layout.paddingTop = parseFloat(style.paddingTop) || 16;
+1233|    state.layout.lineHeight = lineHeight;
+1234|    state.layout.font = style.font || `${fontSize}px ${style.fontFamily}`;
+1235|
+1236|    const firstLineNumber = els.editorInner.querySelector('.line-number');
+1237|    state.layout.gutterWidth = firstLineNumber ? 
firstLineNumber.getBoundingClientRect().width : 0;
+1238|
+1239|    if (measureContext) {
+1240|      measureContext.font = `${fontSize}px ${style.fontFamily}`;
+1241|    }
+1242|  }
+1243|
+1244|  function measureTextWidth(text) {
+1245|    if (!measureContext) {
+1246|      return text.length * 8;
+1247|    }
+1248|    return measureContext.measureText(text).width;
+1249|  }
+1250|
+1251|  function renderEditor() {
+1252|    if (!state.report) {
+1253|      els.editorInner.innerHTML = '<div class="empty-note 
editor-empty">Open a report from the list to begin.</div>';
+1254|      return;
+1255|    }
+1256|
+1257|    const selection = selectionRange();
+1258|    const lines = state.document.lines;
+1259|    const lineCountWidth = Math.max(4, String(lines.length).length);
+1260|    document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--gutter-width', 
`${lineCountWidth + 1.5}ch`);
+1261|
+1262|    const scrollTop = els.editorViewport.scrollTop;
+1263|    const scrollLeft = els.editorViewport.scrollLeft;
+1264|
+1265|    const html = [];
+1266|    for (const line of lines) {
+1267|      const localStart = Math.max(0, selection.start - line.start);
+1268|      const localEnd = Math.min(line.text.length, selection.end - 
line.start);
+1269|      let content = escapeHtml(line.text);
+1270|      if (selection.end > selection.start && localEnd > localStart) {
+1271|        content = `${escapeHtml(line.text.slice(0, localStart))}<span 
class="selection">${escapeHtml(line.text.slice(localStart, 
localEnd))}</span>${escapeHtml(line.text.slice(localEnd))}`;
+1272|      }
+1273|      html.push(`
+1274|        <div class="editor-line" data-line-index="${line.index}">
+1275|          <span class="line-number">${line.index + 1}</span>
+1276|          <span class="line-content">${content || '&#8203;'}</span>
+1277|        </div>
+1278|      `);
+1279|    }
+1280|
+1281|    const caretOffsets = getAllCaretOffsets();
+1282|    for (let index = 0; index < caretOffsets.length; index += 1) {
+1283|      const offset = caretOffsets[index];
+1284|      const position = pixelPositionForOffset(offset);
+1285|      html.push(`<div class="caret ${index === 0 ? 'primary' : 
'secondary'}" style="left:${position.x}px; top:${position.y}px;"></div>`);
+1286|    }
+1287|
+1288|    els.editorInner.innerHTML = html.join('');
+1289|    updateLayoutMetrics();
+1290|
+1291|    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
+1292|      els.editorViewport.scrollTop = scrollTop;
+1293|      els.editorViewport.scrollLeft = scrollLeft;
+1294|      updateLayoutMetrics();
+1295|      renderButtons();
+1296|      renderSaveState();
+1297|    });
+1298|  }
+1299|
+1300|  function pixelPositionForOffset(offset) {
+1301|    const pos = lineColumnPosition(offset);
+1302|    const prefix = pos.line.text.slice(0, 
graphemeOffsetFromColumn(pos.line, pos.column));
+1303|    const x = state.layout.paddingLeft + state.layout.gutterWidth + 
measureTextWidth(prefix) - els.editorViewport.scrollLeft;
+1304|    const y = state.layout.paddingTop + pos.lineIndex * 
state.layout.lineHeight - els.editorViewport.scrollTop;
+1305|    return { x, y };
+1306|  }
+1307|
+1308|  function scrollPrimaryCaretIntoView(forceTop = false) {
+1309|    if (!state.report) {
+1310|      return;
+1311|    }
+1312|    const offset = getPrimaryCaretOffset();
+1313|    const pos = pixelPositionForOffset(offset);
+1314|    const viewport = els.editorViewport;
+1315|    const margin = 48;
+1316|    const caretTop = pos.y;
+1317|    const caretBottom = pos.y + state.layout.lineHeight;
+1318|    const caretLeft = pos.x;
+1319|    const caretRight = pos.x + 2;
+1320|
+1321|    if (forceTop) {
+1322|      viewport.scrollTop = 0;
+1323|      viewport.scrollLeft = 0;
+1324|      return;
+1325|    }
+1326|
+1327|    if (caretTop < viewport.scrollTop + margin) {
+1328|      viewport.scrollTop = Math.max(0, caretTop - margin);
+1329|    } else if (caretBottom > viewport.scrollTop + viewport.clientHeight - 
margin) {
+1330|      viewport.scrollTop = caretBottom - viewport.clientHeight + margin;
+1331|    }
+1332|
+1333|    if (caretLeft < viewport.scrollLeft + margin) {
+1334|      viewport.scrollLeft = Math.max(0, caretLeft - margin);
+1335|    } else if (caretRight > viewport.scrollLeft + viewport.clientWidth - 
margin) {
+1336|      viewport.scrollLeft = caretRight - viewport.clientWidth + margin;
+1337|    }
+1338|  }
+1339|
+1340|  function renderAll() {
+1341|    renderReportHeader();
+1342|    renderReportList();
+1343|    renderHistory();
+1344|    renderPreview();
+1345|    renderCursorState();
+1346|    renderFindCount();
+1347|    renderSaveState();
+1348|    renderButtons();
+1349|    renderEditor();
+1350|  }
+1351|
+1352|  function positionFromClientPoint(clientX, clientY) {
+1353|    const viewportRect = els.editorViewport.getBoundingClientRect();
+1354|    const localX = clientX - viewportRect.left + 
els.editorViewport.scrollLeft - state.layout.paddingLeft - 
state.layout.gutterWidth;
+1355|    const localY = clientY - viewportRect.top + 
els.editorViewport.scrollTop - state.layout.paddingTop;
+1356|    const lineIndex = clamp(Math.floor(localY / state.layout.lineHeight), 
0, state.document.lines.length - 1);
+1357|    const line = state.document.lines[lineIndex];
+1358|    const x = Math.max(0, localX);
+1359|    const column = columnFromX(line, x);
+1360|    return line.start + graphemeOffsetFromColumn(line, column);
+1361|  }
+1362|
+1363|  function columnFromX(line, x) {
+1364|    const boundaries = line.graphemeBoundaries;
+1365|    if (!line.text.length) {
+1366|      return 0;
+1367|    }
+1368|
+1369|    const widths = boundaries.map((boundary) => 
measureTextWidth(line.text.slice(0, boundary)));
+1370|    let low = 0;
+1371|    let high = widths.length - 1;
+1372|    while (low < high) {
+1373|      const mid = Math.floor((low + high) / 2);
+1374|      if (widths[mid] < x) {
+1375|        low = mid + 1;
+1376|      } else {
+1377|        high = mid;
+1378|      }
+1379|    }
+1380|
+1381|    if (low === 0) {
+1382|      return 0;
+1383|    }
+1384|    if (low >= widths.length) {
+1385|      return line.graphemeCount;
+1386|    }
+1387|
+1388|    const prevWidth = widths[low - 1];
+1389|    const currentWidth = widths[low];
+1390|    return (x - prevWidth) < (currentWidth - x) ? low - 1 : low;
+1391|  }
+1392|
+1393|  function selectWordAt(offset) {
+1394|    const matches = state.document.wordSegments.filter((segment) => 
segment.isWordLike && segment.start <= offset && segment.end >= offset);
+1395|    const match = matches[0] || state.document.wordSegments.find((segment)
=> segment.isWordLike && segment.start > offset);
+1396|    if (!match) {
+1397|      collapseTo(offset);
+1398|      return;
+1399|    }
+1400|    selectRange(match.start, match.end);
+1401|  }
+1402|
+1403|  function selectLineAt(offset) {
+1404|    const current = currentLineData(offset);
+1405|    const end = current.line.hasBreak ? current.line.nextStart : 
current.line.end;
+1406|    selectRange(current.line.start, end);
+1407|  }
+1408|
+1409|  function addCaretAt(offset) {
+1410|    const offsets = getAllCaretOffsets();
+1411|    offsets.push(offset);
+1412|    applySelectionAfterEdit(offsets, lineColumnPosition(offset).column);
+1413|  }
+1414|
+1415|  function handlePointerDown(event) {
+1416|    if (event.button !== 0) {
+1417|      return;
+1418|    }
+1419|    if (event.target.closest('button') || event.target.closest('input')) {
+1420|      return;
+1421|    }
+1422|    event.preventDefault();
+1423|    els.editorViewport.focus();
+1424|    updateLayoutMetrics();
+1425|    const offset = positionFromClientPoint(event.clientX, event.clientY);
+1426|
+1427|    if (event.altKey || hasPrimaryModifier(event)) {
+1428|      addCaretAt(offset);
+1429|      state.dragging = false;
+1430|      state.dragMode = 'multi';
+1431|      renderAll();
+1432|      scrollPrimaryCaretIntoView();
+1433|      return;
+1434|    }
+1435|
+1436|    if (event.detail >= 3) {
+1437|      selectLineAt(offset);
+1438|      state.dragging = false;
+1439|      state.dragMode = 'selection';
+1440|      renderAll();
+1441|      scrollPrimaryCaretIntoView();
+1442|      return;
+1443|    }
+1444|
+1445|    if (event.detail === 2) {
+1446|      selectWordAt(offset);
+1447|      state.dragging = false;
+1448|      state.dragMode = 'selection';
+1449|      renderAll();
+1450|      scrollPrimaryCaretIntoView();
+1451|      return;
+1452|    }
+1453|
+1454|    if (event.shiftKey) {
+1455|      const base = state.selection.anchor;
+1456|      selectRange(base, offset);
+1457|      state.dragging = true;
+1458|      state.dragMode = 'selection';
+1459|      state.dragPointerId = event.pointerId;
+1460|      state.dragHasStarted = true;
+1461|      renderAll();
+1462|      return;
+1463|    }
+1464|
+1465|    collapseTo(offset);
+1466|    state.dragging = true;
+1467|    state.dragMode = 'selection';
+1468|    state.dragPointerId = event.pointerId;
+1469|    state.dragHasStarted = true;
+1470|    renderAll();
+1471|  }
+1472|
+1473|  function handlePointerMove(event) {
+1474|    if (!state.dragging || state.dragPointerId !== event.pointerId) {
+1475|      return;
+1476|    }
+1477|    event.preventDefault();
+1478|    const offset = positionFromClientPoint(event.clientX, event.clientY);
+1479|    selectRange(state.selection.anchor, offset);
+1480|    autoScrollForPointer(event.clientX, event.clientY);
+1481|    renderAll();
+1482|  }
+1483|
+1484|  function handlePointerUp(event) {
+1485|    if (state.dragPointerId !== event.pointerId) {
+1486|      return;
+1487|    }
+1488|    state.dragging = false;
+1489|    state.dragPointerId = null;
+1490|    state.dragHasStarted = false;
+1491|    renderAll();
+1492|  }
+1493|
+1494|  function autoScrollForPointer(clientX, clientY) {
+1495|    const rect = els.editorViewport.getBoundingClientRect();
+1496|    const edge = 36;
+1497|    if (clientY < rect.top + edge) {
+1498|      els.editorViewport.scrollTop -= 24;
+1499|    } else if (clientY > rect.bottom - edge) {
+1500|      els.editorViewport.scrollTop += 24;
+1501|    }
+1502|    if (clientX < rect.left + edge) {
+1503|      els.editorViewport.scrollLeft -= 24;
+1504|    } else if (clientX > rect.right - edge) {
+1505|      els.editorViewport.scrollLeft += 24;
+1506|    }
+1507|  }
+1508|
+1509|  function handleDocumentCopy(event) {
+1510|    if (!isEditorFocused()) {
+1511|      return;
+1512|    }
+1513|    const range = getSelectionRange();
+1514|    if (range.start === range.end || state.extraCarets.length) {
+1515|      return;
+1516|    }
+1517|    event.preventDefault();
+1518|    const text = state.text.slice(range.start, range.end);
+1519|    event.clipboardData.setData('text/plain', text);
+1520|  }
+1521|
+1522|  function handleDocumentCut(event) {
+1523|    if (!isEditorFocused()) {
+1524|      return;
+1525|    }
+1526|    const range = getSelectionRange();
+1527|    if (range.start === range.end || state.extraCarets.length) {
+1528|      return;
+1529|    }
+1530|    event.preventDefault();
+1531|    event.clipboardData.setData('text/plain', 
state.text.slice(range.start, range.end));
+1532|    deleteRange(range.start, range.end, 'cut');
+1533|  }
+1534|
+1535|  function handleDocumentPaste(event) {
+1536|    if (!isEditorFocused()) {
+1537|      return;
+1538|    }
+1539|    const text = event.clipboardData.getData('text/plain');
+1540|    if (!text) {
+1541|      return;
+1542|    }
+1543|    event.preventDefault();
+1544|    insertText(text, 'paste');
+1545|  }
+1546|
+1547|  function handleEditorKeyDown(event) {
+1548|    if (!state.report) {
+1549|      return;
+1550|    }
+1551|
+1552|    if (hasPrimaryModifier(event) && event.key.toLowerCase() === 'f') {
+1553|      event.preventDefault();
+1554|      els.findInput.focus();
+1555|      els.findInput.select();
+1556|      return;
+1557|    }
+1558|
+1559|    if (event.key === 'F3') {
+1560|      event.preventDefault();
+1561|      navigateMatches(event.shiftKey ? -1 : 1);
+1562|      return;
+1563|    }
+1564|
+1565|    if (hasPrimaryModifier(event) && event.key.toLowerCase() === 's') {
+1566|      event.preventDefault();
+1567|      saveReport();
+1568|      return;
+1569|    }
+1570|
+1571|    if (hasPrimaryModifier(event) && event.key.toLowerCase() === 'a') {
+1572|      event.preventDefault();
+1573|      selectAll();
+1574|      return;
+1575|    }
+1576|
+1577|    if (hasPrimaryModifier(event) && event.key.toLowerCase() === 'z') {
+1578|      event.preventDefault();
+1579|      if (event.shiftKey) {
+1580|        redo();
+1581|      } else {
+1582|        undo();
+1583|      }
+1584|      return;
+1585|    }
+1586|
+1587|    if (hasPrimaryModifier(event) && event.key.toLowerCase() === 'y') {
+1588|      event.preventDefault();
+1589|      redo();
+1590|      return;
+1591|    }
+1592|
+1593|    if (event.key === 'Escape') {
+1594|      event.preventDefault();
+1595|      els.findInput.focus();
+1596|      return;
+1597|    }
+1598|
+1599|    if (event.key === 'Tab') {
+1600|      event.preventDefault();
+1601|      indentSelection(event.shiftKey);
+1602|      return;
+1603|    }
+1604|
+1605|    if (event.key === 'Enter') {
+1606|      event.preventDefault();
+1607|      insertText('\n', 'typing');
+1608|      return;
+1609|    }
+1610|
+1611|    if (event.key === 'Backspace') {
+1612|      event.preventDefault();
+1613|      if (hasPrimaryModifier(event)) {
+1614|        deleteByWord(-1);
+1615|      } else {
+1616|        deleteBackward('delete');
+1617|      }
+1618|      return;
+1619|    }
+1620|
+1621|    if (event.key === 'Delete') {
+1622|      event.preventDefault();
+1623|      if (hasPrimaryModifier(event)) {
+1624|        deleteByWord(1);
+1625|      } else {
+1626|        deleteForward('delete');
+1627|      }
+1628|      return;
+1629|    }
+1630|
+1631|    if (event.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
+1632|      event.preventDefault();
+1633|      moveSelection(-1, event.shiftKey, hasPrimaryModifier(event), false);
+1634|      return;
+1635|    }
+1636|
+1637|    if (event.key === 'ArrowRight') {
+1638|      event.preventDefault();
+1639|      moveSelection(1, event.shiftKey, hasPrimaryModifier(event), false);
+1640|      return;
+1641|    }
+1642|
+1643|    if (event.key === 'ArrowUp') {
+1644|      event.preventDefault();
+1645|      moveSelection(-1, event.shiftKey, false, true);
+1646|      return;
+1647|    }
+1648|
+1649|    if (event.key === 'ArrowDown') {
+1650|      event.preventDefault();
+1651|      moveSelection(1, event.shiftKey, false, true);
+1652|      return;
+1653|    }
+1654|
+1655|    if (event.key === 'Home') {
+1656|      event.preventDefault();
+1657|      moveToLineEdge('start', event.shiftKey);
+1658|      return;
+1659|    }
+1660|
+1661|    if (event.key === 'End') {
+1662|      event.preventDefault();
+1663|      moveToLineEdge('end', event.shiftKey);
+1664|      return;
+1665|    }
+1666|
+1667|    if (event.key.length === 1 && !event.ctrlKey && !event.metaKey) {
+1668|      if (event.key === '\r') {
+1669|        return;
+1670|      }
+1671|      event.preventDefault();
+1672|      insertText(event.key, 'typing');
+1673|    }
+1674|  }
+1675|
+1676|  function deleteByWord(direction) {
+1677|    const offsets = getAllCaretOffsets();
+1678|    const edits = [];
+1679|    for (const offset of offsets) {
+1680|      const boundary = direction < 0 ? previousWordBoundary(offset) : 
nextWordBoundary(offset);
+1681|      if (boundary !== offset) {
+1682|        if (direction < 0) {
+1683|          edits.push({ start: boundary, end: offset, insert: '' });
+1684|        } else {
+1685|          edits.push({ start: offset, end: boundary, insert: '' });
+1686|        }
+1687|      }
+1688|    }
+1689|    applyBatchEdits(edits, 'deleteWord');
+1690|  }
+1691|
+1692|  function previousWordBoundary(offset) {
+1693|    const segments = state.document.wordSegments;
+1694|    for (let index = segments.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
+1695|      const segment = segments[index];
+1696|      if (segment.isWordLike && segment.end < offset) {
+1697|        return segment.start;
+1698|      }
+1699|      if (segment.isWordLike && segment.start < offset && segment.end >= 
offset) {
+1700|        return segment.start;
+1701|      }
+1702|    }
+1703|    return 0;
+1704|  }
+1705|
+1706|  function nextWordBoundary(offset) {
+1707|    const segments = state.document.wordSegments;
+1708|    for (const segment of segments) {
+1709|      if (segment.isWordLike && segment.start > offset) {
+1710|        return segment.start;
+1711|      }
+1712|      if (segment.isWordLike && segment.start <= offset && segment.end > 
offset) {
+1713|        return segment.end;
+1714|      }
+1715|    }
+1716|    return state.document.text.length;
+1717|  }
+1718|
+1719|  function moveToLineEdge(edge, withShift) {
+1720|    endEditSession();
+1721|    state.extraCarets = [];
+1722|    const current = currentLineData(getPrimaryCaretOffset());
+1723|    const next = edge === 'start'
+1724|      ? current.line.start
+1725|      : current.line.hasBreak
+1726|        ? current.line.nextStart
+1727|        : current.line.end;
+1728|    if (withShift) {
+1729|      state.selection.focus = next;
+1730|    } else {
+1731|      state.selection = { anchor: next, focus: next };
+1732|    }
+1733|    state.preferredColumn = edge === 'start' ? 0 : 
current.line.graphemeCount;
+1734|    renderAll();
+1735|    scrollPrimaryCaretIntoView();
+1736|  }
+1737|
+1738|  function undo() {
+1739|    if (!state.undoStack.length) {
+1740|      setFeedback('Nothing to undo.', 'info');
+1741|      return;
+1742|    }
+1743|    state.redoStack.push({
+1744|      text: state.text,
+1745|      selection: cloneSelection(state.selection),
+1746|      extraCarets: [...state.extraCarets],
+1747|      preferredColumn: state.preferredColumn,
+1748|    });
+1749|    const snapshot = state.undoStack.pop();
+1750|    restoreSnapshot(snapshot);
+1751|    setFeedback('Undid the last edit.', 'info');
+1752|  }
+1753|
+1754|  function redo() {
+1755|    if (!state.redoStack.length) {
+1756|      setFeedback('Nothing to redo.', 'info');
+1757|      return;
+1758|    }
+1759|    state.undoStack.push({
+1760|      text: state.text,
+1761|      selection: cloneSelection(state.selection),
+1762|      extraCarets: [...state.extraCarets],
+1763|      preferredColumn: state.preferredColumn,
+1764|    });
+1765|    const snapshot = state.redoStack.pop();
+1766|    restoreSnapshot(snapshot);
+1767|    setFeedback('Redid the last edit.', 'info');
+1768|  }
+1769|
+1770|  function handleFindInputKeyDown(event) {
+1771|    if (event.key === 'Escape') {
+1772|      event.preventDefault();
+1773|      els.editorViewport.focus();
+1774|      return;
+1775|    }

   653    function copySelectionToClipboard(cut = false) {
   654      const range = getSelectionRange();
   655      if (range.start === range.end || state.extraCarets.length) {
   656        return false;
   657      }
   658      const text = state.text.slice(range.start, range.end);

   661          deleteRange(range.start, range.end, 'cut');
   662        }
   663      });

   666    async function writeClipboardText(text) {
   667      try {
   668        if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {
   669          await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
   670          return true;

   672      } catch {

      function indentSelection(unindent = false) {
        endEditSession();
        const range = getSelectionRange();
        const lines = state.document.lines;
        const firstLineIndex = currentLineData(range.start).lineIndex;
        const lastLineIndex = currentLineData(Math.max(range.end - 1, 
0)).lineIndex;
        const edits = [];
        let anchorShift = 0;
        let focusShift = 0;
    
        for (let index = firstLineIndex; index <= lastLineIndex; index += 1) {
          const line = lines[index];
          if (unindent) {
            if (line.text.startsWith('\t')) {
              edits.push({ start: line.start, end: line.start + 1, insert: '' 
});
              if (line.start < range.start) anchorShift -= 1;
              if (line.start < range.end) focusShift -= 1;
            } else if (line.text.startsWith('  ')) {
              edits.push({ start: line.start, end: line.start + 2, insert: '' 
});
              if (line.start < range.start) anchorShift -= 2;
              if (line.start < range.end) focusShift -= 2;
            }
          } else {
            edits.push({ start: line.start, end: line.start, insert: '\t' });
            if (line.start <= range.start) anchorShift += 1;
            if (line.start <= range.end) focusShift += 1;
          }
        }
    
        if (!edits.length) {
          return;
        }
    
        const sorted = edits.sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start);
        let nextText = state.text;
        let shift = 0;
        for (const edit of sorted) {
          const start = edit.start + shift;
          const end = edit.end + shift;
          nextText = replaceRangeInText(nextText, start, end, edit.insert);
          shift += edit.insert.length - (edit.end - edit.start);
        }
    
        replaceText(
          nextText,
          {
            anchor: range.start + anchorShift,
            focus: range.end + focusShift,
          },
          unindent ? 'unindent' : 'indent',
        );
      }
    

  new_str: 
      function indentSelection(unindent = false) {
        endEditSession();
        const hasRangeSelection = !selectionIsCollapsed() && 
state.extraCarets.length === 0;
    
        if (!hasRangeSelection) {
          const offsets = getAllCaretOffsets();
          if (!unindent) {
            applyBatchEdits(offsets.map((offset) => ({ start: offset, end: 
offset, insert: '\t' })), 'indent');
            return;
          }
    
          const edits = [];
          const seen = new Set();
          for (const offset of offsets) {
            const line = currentLineData(offset).line;
            const key = `${line.start}:${line.end}`;
            if (seen.has(key)) {
              continue;
            }
            seen.add(key);
            if (line.text.startsWith('\t')) {
              edits.push({ start: line.start, end: line.start + 1, insert: '' 
});
            } else if (line.text.startsWith('  ')) {
              edits.push({ start: line.start, end: line.start + 2, insert: '' 
});
            }
          }
    
          if (!edits.length) {
            return;
          }
          applyBatchEdits(edits, 'unindent');
          return;
        }
    
        const range = getSelectionRange();
        const lines = state.document.lines;
        const firstLineIndex = currentLineData(range.start).lineIndex;
        const lastLineIndex = currentLineData(Math.max(range.end - 1, 
0)).lineIndex;
        const edits = [];
        let anchorShift = 0;
        let focusShift = 0;
    
        for (let index = firstLineIndex; index <= lastLineIndex; index += 1) {
          const line = lines[index];
          if (unindent) {
            if (line.text.startsWith('\t')) {
              edits.push({ start: line.start, end: line.start + 1, insert: '' 
});
              if (line.start < range.start) anchorShift -= 1;
              if (line.start < range.end) focusShift -= 1;
            } else if (line.text.startsWith('  ')) {
              edits.push({ start: line.start, end: line.start + 2, insert: '' 
});
              if (line.start < range.start) anchorShift -= 2;
              if (line.start < range.end) focusShift -= 2;
            }
          } else {
            edits.push({ start: line.start, end: line.start, insert: '\t' });
            if (line.start <= range.start) anchorShift += 1;
            if (line.start <= range.end) focusShift += 1;
          }
        }
    
        if (!edits.length) {
          return;
        }
    
        const sorted = edits.sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start);
        let nextText = state.text;
        let shift = 0;
        for (const edit of sorted) {
          const start = edit.start + shift;
          const end = edit.end + shift;
          nextText = replaceRangeInText(nextText, start, end, edit.insert);
          shift += edit.insert.length - (edit.end - edit.start);
        }
    
        replaceText(
          nextText,
          {
            anchor: range.start + anchorShift,
            focus: range.end + focusShift,
          },
          unindent ? 'unindent' : 'indent',
        );
      }
    

  kind: "FileEditorAction"

  old_str: 
      function activateMatch(index, scrollIntoView = false) {
        if (!state.matches.length) {
          return;
        }
        const match = state.matches[clamp(index, 0, state.matches.length - 1)];
        if (!match) {
          return;
        }
        state.activeMatchIndex = index;
        state.selection = { anchor: match.start, focus: match.end };
        state.extraCarets = [];
        state.preferredColumn = lineColumnPosition(match.end).column;
        renderAll();
        if (scrollIntoView) {
          scrollPrimaryCaretIntoView();
        }
      }

      function activateMatch(index, scrollIntoView = false) {
        if (!state.matches.length) {
          return;
        }
        endEditSession();
        const match = state.matches[clamp(index, 0, state.matches.length - 1)];
        if (!match) {
          return;
        }
        state.activeMatchIndex = index;
        state.selection = { anchor: match.start, focus: match.end };
        state.extraCarets = [];
        state.preferredColumn = lineColumnPosition(match.end).column;
        renderAll();
        if (scrollIntoView) {
          scrollPrimaryCaretIntoView();
        }
      }
    

  kind: "FileEditorAction"
